PHYSICALLY CONSTRAINED GAUSSIAN RECONSTRUCTION / MILESTONE 2

Learning when stochastic motion is needed

Matched deterministic, fixed-noise and adaptive-mixture inference across independent motion/noise realizations and observation schedules. All methods use the same image linearization, scene prior, material model and input images. Noise levels are compared by integrating latent Gaussian disturbances, using observed images only.

Scope: one known rest geometry and camera path. These are independent realizations, not independent scene geometries. Scale evidence is exact for a local linearized likelihood and approximate for the nonlinear renderer. The deterministic pulse condition contains an external force absent from inference: selected noise can reflect model mismatch. This is not a demonstrated physical-noise detector or across-scene calibration result.

All tables score every withheld timestamp. Schedules have identical image budgets. Error bars show seed standard deviations; they are not confidence intervals. The coverage plot averages per-realization diagnostics across the listed schedules; repeated schedules for one seed are correlated.

AUSE assesses error ranking, not probability calibration. Coverage must be read with interval width and CRPS. The last column measures nonlinear render disagreement with the fitted local likelihood, in units of known image-noise sigma; a large value weakens confidence in the approximate evidence. Noise-grid and spike-prior choices are declared before evaluation; no held-out trajectory or image is used to select them. The complete record includes sensitivity to alternative predeclared zero-noise prior masses.
